Modbus/TCP and FHIR/HL7 support realistic communication across healthcare and industrial components. Experimental evaluation shows low computation overhead, with average normalized CPU utilization below 0.4 % per container and most lightweight services operating below 0.01%. OpenPLC Modbus TCP operations achieve a median round-trip latency of 0.901 ms. The testbed also captures a multi-stage SSH-based attack propagating from the DMZ to the IT and PLC networks. The framework provides a foundation for extending the emulated environment toward a hardware-enabled hospital digital twin.

</details>


### [80] [MobileWorldSafety: Benchmarking GUI Agent Safety Against Environmental Injection Attacks in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17659)
*Sujin Chen,Lijun Li,Tianyi Du,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MobileWorldSafety是一个基于真实Android应用的基准测试集，包含142个风险任务，用于系统评估GUI智能体在环境注入攻击下的安全性。实验表明，六种主流智能体的攻击成功率介于40.4%至66.9%，所有智能体均高度脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的GUI智能体在处理不可信环境内容时极易受到环境注入攻击（包括间接提示注入和对抗性指令），但现有基准未能覆盖日常移动使用场景，缺乏对移动端GUI智能体在环境注入攻击下的系统评估。

Method: 构建包含142个风险任务的基准集，每个任务基于真实Android应用，并定义程序可验证的风险指标（基于最终系统状态）。采用两阶段评估流水线：规则验证处理明确案例，LLM法官裁决模糊案例，以区分安全失败与能力失败。评估对象包括通用智能体和专用GUI智能体共六种。

Result: 六种被评估智能体的攻击成功率范围为40.4%至66.9%，所有智能体均表现出高度脆弱性，说明当前智能体在对抗性内容以日常移动上下文呈现时，往往无法维持安全对齐。

Conclusion: MobileWorldSafety为量化移动GUI智能体的安全漏洞提供了基础，表明现有智能体在面对环境注入攻击时存在显著安全缺陷，需要进一步研究以开发鲁棒的移动GUI智能体。

Abstract: LLM-powered GUI agents that autonomously operate smartphones are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to early real-world deployment. However, because these agents routinely process untrusted environmental content, they are highly vulnerable to environmental injection attacks, which include indirect prompt injections and adversarial instructions. Such attacks can manipulate the behavior of agents without user awareness through diverse channels encountered in everyday mobile use. Despite these risks, existing benchmarks often fail to capture everyday user scenarios, lacking a systematic evaluation of GUI agents under environmental injection attacks on mobile devices. To address this gap, we introduce MobileWorldSafety, a benchmark of 142 risk tasks built on real Android applications. For each task, we define a programmatically verifiable risk indicator over the final system state and evaluate outcomes with a two-stage pipeline: rule-based verification handles unambiguous cases, while an LLM judge adjudicates ambiguous ones. This distinguishes safety failures from capability failures and enables objective and reproducible assessment. Evaluations on six agents, including both general agents and specialized GUI agents, demonstrate that all agents remain highly vulnerable, with attack success rates ranging from 40.4% to 66.9%. These findings indicate that current agents often fail to maintain safety alignment when adversarial content is presented as ordinary mobile context. MobileWorldSafety provides a foundation for quantifying these vulnerabilities and advancing research on robust mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [81] [MemCatalyst: Amplifying Data Auditing on Vision-Language Models via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17722)
*Xukun Luan,Jinyan Liu,Yuhui Gong,Yuanguo Bi,Bing Hu,Xuesong Li,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MemCatalyst，一种数据投毒工具，用于增强视觉-语言模型（VLM）上的成员推断（MI）审计性能。通过文本投毒（PT）和图像投毒（PI）两种策略，迫使VLM在训练中过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的不一致性，从而提升数据审计对成员信息的检测能力。实验表明该方法在少量投毒样本预算下显著提升MI AUC分数，且对模型性能影响极小，并具备跨VLM架构的黑盒可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据持有者（如艺术家）急需判断其数据是否未经授权被用于VLM训练，这涉及知识产权和个人隐私问题。现有的数据审计方法需要更强的手段来有效地检测VLM训练中数据的使用情况。

Method: MemCatalyst采用两种数据投毒策略：文本投毒（PT）和图像投毒（PI），通过在训练过程中迫使VLM过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的特定不一致性，使模型对成员信息审计更加敏感。该方法还展示了投毒样本在不同VLM架构间的黑盒可迁移性。

Result: 在两个主流VLM上使用五种最先进的数据审计方法进行广泛评估，MemCatalyst在极小的投毒样本预算下显著提升了MI AUC分数，同时对模型性能的影响可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: MemCatalyst是一种有效且高效的数据审计增强工具，能够在低预算条件下大幅提升VLM成员推断的检测能力，同时几乎不损害模型性能，并在黑盒环境下具备跨架构的迁移能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) achieve outstanding performance largely due to the amount of training data available on the internet. At the same time, data holders (e.g., artists) urgently need to determine whether their data has been used for model training without authorization, which concerns both intellectual property rights and personal privacy. Data auditing, particularly through membership inference (MI), has attracted attention as a direct tool. This work proposes MemCatalyst, a set of data poisoning tools, aiming to amplify the data auditing performance on VLMs. MemCatalyst employs two strategies: Poisoning Text (PT) and Poisoning Image (PI). MemCatalyst forces VLMs to over-learn specific inconsistencies between image features and textual semantics during training, thereby increasing their susceptibility to membership information auditing. Crucially, the transferability of poisoned samples across different VLM architectures is demonstrated to be effective in the black-box setting. Extensive evaluations using five state-of-the-art data audits on two prominent VLMs demonstrate that MemCatalyst markedly enhances MI AUC scores with a minimal budget of poisoned samples, while maintaining a negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [82] [BullsEye: Directed Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17729)
*Lorenzo Ralli,Emilio Coppa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BULLSEYE，首个以基本块级距离调度闭源Linux固件模糊测试的定向灰盒模糊测试（DGF）框架，在40个漏洞点（32个固件镜像）上全部再现目标，相比最强基线快9.5x-72.5x（几何均值），相比GREENHOUSE快9.8x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备广泛部署扩大了数字攻击面，固件中频繁复用第三方软件组件常引入已知漏洞。公开的PoC漏洞利用使确定固件镜像是否暴露漏洞变得紧迫。现有DGF技术仅能在函数粒度上处理固件，粒度太粗，无法精确瞄准漏洞所在的代码块。

Method: BULLSEYE结合静态与动态分析，在资源受限的固件域中实现基本块级距离引导的DGF。提出新的DGF启发式策略，弥补传统方法的局限，支持对用户指定目标（漏洞基本块）的精确调度。

Result: 在40个漏洞点（32个固件镜像）上，BULLSEYE在预算内再现全部目标；最强的四个基线中只有35个目标被再现。相比基线，Time-to-Exposure降低9.5x至72.5x（几何均值）；相比GREENHOUSE（其管道支持的18个目标），BULLSEYE快9.8x（几何均值）。

Conclusion: BULLSEYE是首个在基本块粒度上对闭源Linux固件实施定向灰盒模糊测试的框架，显著提升了固件中第三方组件已知漏洞的检测效率与精度，验证了基本块级距离引导在固件模糊测试中的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) devices has expanded the digital attack surface, making firmware analysis critical for modern software security. A key security concern stems from the frequent reuse of third-party software components, a practice that often introduces known vulnerabilities into firmware images. Whether a given image actually exposes such a flaw is an open question, and public proof-of concept exploits make answering it urgent. Directed Greybox Fuzzing (DGF), a technique that enables targeted exploration of specific binary locations, offers a promising solution for detecting such vulnerabilities. However, DGF has reached firmware only at function granularity, too coarse to aim at the vulnerable block itself.
  This article presents BULLSEYE, the first DGF framework to schedule closed-source Linux-based firmware fuzzing by basic-block-level distance to user-specified targets. Our methodology combines static and dynamic analysis to enable DGF in the constrained firmware domain, focusing on vulnerabilities in reused third-party components. We introduce novel DGF heuristics that address limitations of traditional approaches. We compare BULLSEYE against four greybox-fuzzing baselines sharing its execution back-end, including reimplementations of AFLGO and WINDRANGER, and against GREENHOUSE, a state-of-the-art firmware re-hosting framework. On 40 vulnerability sites across 32 firmware images, BULLSEYE reproduces every target within budget, against 35 for the strongest of the four baselines, and reduces Time-to-Exposure by a geometric mean of 9.5x to 72.5x over them; against GREENHOUSE, on the 18 targets its pipeline supports, BULLSEYE is faster by a geometric mean of 9.8x.

</details>


### [83] [Achievement Unlocked: Let's Get Hacked! An Empirical Study of Cybercrime in the Video Gaming Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17754)
*Janine Schneider,Jan Kallenborn,Tim Hoffmann,Maximilian Eichhorn,Thorsten Holz,Bhupendra Acharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法（问卷调查、访谈和公开帖子分析）对游戏生态系统中的网络犯罪进行了实证研究，发现数字物品窃取是主要攻击动机，游戏特定功能（如物品交易、团队投票、锦标赛）促使玩家参与高风险互动，并揭示了客户支持流程中的漏洞和安全机制的虚假安全感问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频游戏行业庞大用户群使其成为复杂的社交经济生态系统，但网络犯罪分子利用游戏特定机制攻击玩家。尽管威胁日益增长，游戏生态系统中的网络犯罪在先前研究中缺乏系统性的关注。

Method: 采用定性分析与观察性分析相结合的方法：对57名国际参与者进行在线调查，对两名已确认的游戏网络犯罪受害者进行半结构化访谈，并分析来自多个在线游戏平台的2,574条公开报告网络犯罪事件的帖子。

Result: 研究发现数字物品窃取是攻击的主要动机；游戏相关功能和服务（如物品交易、团队投票、锦标赛）促使玩家参与高风险互动；攻击者有针对性地利用客户支持流程中的弱点；某些安全机制仅提供了虚假的保护感。

Conclusion: 游戏特定机制和客户支持缺陷是网络犯罪的关键驱动因素，现有安全措施存在不足，需要系统性地改进游戏平台的安全设计和客户支持流程以有效保护玩家。

Abstract: The ubiquity of the video game industry and its large user base have transformed video games into complex social and economic ecosystems. Unfortunately, this growing popularity also attracts cybercriminals who deliberately exploit game-specific mechanisms to target players. Despite this growing threat, cybercrime in the gaming ecosystem has received little systematic attention in prior research.
  In this work, we present an empirical study of cybercrime affecting video game players, combining qualitative and observational analyses to characterize gaming-related attacks, identify common attack vectors and motivations, and examine player responses. Our study is based on an online survey with 57 international participants, semi-structured interviews with two confirmed victims of gaming-related cybercrime, and an analysis of 2,574 publicly available posts reporting cybercrime incidents across multiple online gaming platforms. Our findings indicate that the theft of digital items is a prevalent motivation for attacks. We further observe that gaming-related features and services, such as item trading, team voting, and tournaments, create incentives for players to engage in risky interactions. In addition, our results highlight the targeted exploitation of weaknesses in customer support processes and reveal that certain security mechanisms provide only a false sense of protection.

</details>


### [84] [Efficient Fuzzy PSI under One-Sided Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17770)
*Xinpeng Yang,Meng Hao,Yanxue Jia,Chenkai Weng,Yonggang Wen,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个在单侧假设下、针对一般L_{p∈[1,∞]}距离的高效模糊私有集合交集（fuzzy PSI）协议，仅依赖轻量级对称密钥原语，并通过前缀字典树技术将复杂度从O((logδ)^d)或O(δ)降至O(logδ)。实验表明该方法在计算和通信效率上相比现有工作实现了数百倍甚至数千倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模糊PSI协议在处理一般Minkowski距离时，要么依赖于强两侧几何分离假设，要么在单侧假设下产生高昂开销，缺乏具体高效的实现方案。

Method: 作者在单侧假设下，仅使用轻量级对称密钥原语构建模糊PSI协议，同时支持发送方侧和接收方侧两种设置；针对稀疏输入分布设计了更高效的专门协议；并创新性地将前缀字典树（prefix trie）技术融入协议中，将复杂度从O((logδ)^d)或O(δ)降低到O(logδ)。

Result: 在广泛参数设置下的实验中，相较于van Baarsen和Pu (EUROCRYPT'24)的工作，实现了高达239倍的加速和20倍的通信降低；相较于Dang等(CCS'25)的工作，实现了高达518倍的加速和63倍的通信降低；相较于Bui等(ASIACRYPT'25)的工作，实现了高达4818倍的加速和282倍的通信降低。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在单侧假设下实现对一般L_{p∈[1,∞]}距离的具体高效模糊PSI协议，通过前缀字典树技术大幅降低了随阈值δ增长的开销，在计算和通信效率上全面超越现有方案。

Abstract: Fuzzy private set intersection (PSI) enables two parties to identify approximately matching elements between their input sets, where two elements are considered a match if their distance is at most a threshold $δ$ under a given metric. Although substantial progress has been made, existing constructions for general Minkowski distances either rely on strong two-sided geometric separation assumptions or incur substantial overhead under one-sided assumptions.
  In this work, we present the first concretely efficient fuzzy PSI protocols for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances under one-sided assumptions, relying solely on lightweight symmetric-key primitives. Our constructions support both sender-sided and receiver-sided settings. We further study sparser input distributions and present more efficient protocols tailored to this case. To reduce the overhead scaling with $δ$, we non-trivially incorporate prefix trie techniques into our protocols, achieving $O(\logδ)$ complexity for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances for the first time, improving upon $O((\logδ)^d)$ or $O(δ)$ complexities of prior works.
  Extensive experiments, across a wide range of parameter settings, show that our protocols significantly outperform prior works under the same assumptions. Specifically, against van Baarsen and Pu (EUROCRYPT'24), our protocols achieve up to $239\times$ faster computation and up to $20\times$ lower communication. Against Dang et al. (CCS'25), we achieve up to $518\times$ speedup and up to $63\times$ communication reduction. Against Bui et al. (ASIACRYPT'25), we achieve up to $4818\times$ faster computation and up to $282\times$ lower communication.

</details>


### [85] [Diff-DDoS: Realistic Cyber-Physical Attack Synthesis and Robust Detection for 5G-Enabled CPS Using Tabular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17796)
*Bilal Hussain,Xiao Tang,Qinghe Du,Tan Li,Muhammad Azhar,Danista Khan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-DDoS框架，利用表格扩散模型（TabDDPM）和对抗扩散训练（ADT）在数据稀缺的5G网络环境中合成真实分布保持的攻击样本，用于压力测试和强化DDoS检测器。实验表明，在Milano CDR数据集上，ResNet50配合ADT能将F1分数从灾难性下降恢复至79.62%-100%，显著优于CTGAN等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于深度学习的5G网络DDoS检测器面临标注攻击数据稀缺和合成样本不真实的双重挑战。使用固定缩放乘法的手工合成攻击训练的检测器在遇到真实分布保持样本时性能灾难性下降（F1分数下降约47%-100%），亟需一种能生成真实、分布保持攻击样本来暴露和加固检测器脆弱性的方法。

Method: 提出三阶段Diff-DDoS框架：(1) Phase 1基于CDR（呼叫详细记录）的时空网格训练基线CNN小区级检测器；(2) Phase 2使用TabDDPM（表格去噪扩散概率模型）在正常CDR聚合数据上生成逼真攻击样本，暴露检测器漏洞；(3) Phase 3引入对抗扩散训练（ADT），通过逆分类器引导生成困难但分布保持的样本，迭代训练检测器直至收敛。

Result: 在Milano CDR数据集上覆盖SMS泛洪、静默呼叫、互联网信令和混合场景，ResNet50结合ADT恢复F1分数：静默呼叫79.62%、互联网100%、混合92.79%。经验证集阈值校准后，ADT在SMS场景达到100% F1（对比CTGAN仅47.3%），在静默呼叫场景与最强梯度对抗训练基线持平。

Conclusion: 表格扩散模型为数据稀缺的5G网络物理系统中入侵检测器的压力测试和加固提供了有效工具，显著优于传统生成对抗网络（如CTGAN）基线，能够生成分布保持的对抗样本并提升检测器鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep learning-based DDoS detectors for 5G-enabled cyber-physical systems face scarce labeled attack data and unrealistic synthetic substitutes, which limit robustness against adaptive adversaries. Detectors trained on hand-crafted attacks with fixed scaling multipliers degrade catastrophically (F1-score drops of about 47 percent to 100 percent, depending on scenario) when confronted with realistic, distribution-preserving samples. We propose Diff-DDoS, a three-phase framework for realistic attack synthesis and robust detection using tabular diffusion models. Phase 1 trains a baseline CNN cell-level detector on spatiotemporal grids from call detail records (CDRs). Phase 2 trains a tabular denoising diffusion probabilistic model (TabDDPM) on normal CDR aggregates to generate realistic attacks and expose detector vulnerabilities. Phase 3 introduces adversarial diffusion training (ADT), using inverse classifier guidance to generate hard yet distribution-preserving samples until the detector converges. On a Milano CDR dataset across SMS-flooding, silent-call, Internet-signaling, and blended scenarios, ResNet50 with ADT recovers F1-scores of 79.62 percent (silent-call), 100 percent (Internet), and 92.79 percent (blended). After validation-based threshold calibration, ADT reaches 100 percent SMS F1 versus 47.3 percent for CTGAN, and matches the strongest gradient-based adversarial-training baseline on silent-call. These results support tabular diffusion models for stress-testing and hardening intrusion detectors in data-scarce 5G cyber-physical deployments.

</details>


### [86] [The Model's Tell: Measuring Context-Leakage Attack Signals with Behavior Gauges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17829)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Boting Lu,Wenyue Li,Xiaoping Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Jie Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LeakGauge，一种通过探测模型prefill阶段token概率来检测上下文泄露攻击风险的方法。在不依赖隐藏状态提取的前提下，通过在输入末尾追加一个内容无关的"泄露行为指示"后缀，将其token概率映射为攻击风险分数。在11个LLM（包括GLM-5.2 753B和Kimi-K3 2.8T）上，对未见攻击达到0.944-0.996的AUROC，且对语言变化和语义泄露保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越依赖外部上下文（如系统提示或检索文档）来提升生成质量，但这些上下文与用户查询一起处理时产生了攻击面：对抗性输入可以诱导模型泄露这些内容。先前的探测研究表明泄露相关信号存在于隐藏状态中，但提取隐藏状态在部署上存在额外挑战。因此，本文旨在探索这些内部信号是否在解码前留下一个更易获取的"特征"（tell）。

Method: LeakGauge的核心方法包括：(1) 在输入末尾追加一个"指示后缀"（gauge suffix），用于探测泄露行为；(2) 将prefill阶段的token概率映射为攻击风险分数。直接指示使用机密内容的初始token，但作者发现内容无关的（content-agnostic）指示——即用语言形式表达泄露行为——能产生更稳健的信号。此外，通过激活干预（activation-steering）实验验证风险分数对内部泄露相关方向敏感。

Result: 在11个LLM上（包括GLM-5.2 753B和Kimi-K3 2.8T），LeakGauge在未见攻击上达到AUROC 0.944-0.996。信号在内容语言变化或攻击从逐字泄露转变为语义泄露时依然稳定。该方案可实现为输入检测器，仅需少于0.5K额外参数，额外延迟10.34ms。

Conclusion: LeakGauge证明了解码前的可观测信号（token概率）足以作为上下文泄露风险的可靠指标，不需要访问隐藏状态。内容无关的指示设计提供了更强的泛化能力，且该信号与模型内部泄露相关的表示方向存在可解释的关联，为LLM上下文泄露防御提供了一种轻量级、高效的实用方案。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly rely on external contexts, such as pre-defined system prompts or retrieved documents, to improve generation quality. However, processing these contexts alongside user queries creates an attack surface: adversarial inputs can induce models to disclose them. Prior probing studies suggest that leakage-related signals emerge in hidden states, yet the need to extract these states poses additional deployment challenges. In this paper, we explore whether this internal signal leaves a more accessible ``tell'' before decoding. We propose LeakGauge, which probes this response by appending a suffix that gauges leakage behavior and mapping its prefill token probabilities to an attack-risk score. While a direct gauge uses the initial tokens of confidential content, we find that a content-agnostic one that verbalizes leakage behavior yields more robust signals. Across 11 LLMs, including GLM-5.2 (753B) and Kimi-K3 (2.8T), LeakGauge reaches an AUROC range of 0.944--0.996 on unseen attacks. The signal remains stable when the content changes language or the attack shifts from verbatim to semantic disclosure. By activation-steering interventions, we further show that the risk score is sensitive to an internal leakage-related direction, relating the observable signal to the model's internal representation. In addition, LeakGauge enables an input detector with fewer than 0.5K extra parameters and added latency of 10.34 ms. Code: \href{https://github.com/yeasen-z/LeakGauge}.

</details>


### [87] [COMA: A Compositional Misleading Attack Class on Security-RAG, and a Causal Counterfactual Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17960)
*Chinmay Gondhalekar,Urjitkumar Patel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究安全运营中心（SOC）中分析师面向的专业副驾驶（copilot）所依赖的检索增强生成（RAG）系统的一类新型攻击——组合性误导（COMA）。即使每个对抗文档在事实上正确、无指令、非矛盾且分布良性，通过文档间的"组合"仍然可以误导系统将正确诊断的漏洞引向低劣的修复方案，或翻转可利用性判定。作者提出因果反事实防御（CCD）审计方法，通过留一法测量每个检索文档的因果影响力，并标记影响力集中于低信任文档的回答，有效定位攻击且无误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设攻击需要注入虚假、含指令或具有明显对抗特征的文档，但安全副驾驶的RAG系统在"每个文档本身都无可指摘"的情况下仍可能被组合性误导，这是一个被忽视且高度危险的漏洞面，需要在大型语言模型背景下系统化研究。

Method: 提出COMA攻击框架，包含两种攻击变体：（1）行动腐败（action-corruption），将正确诊断的漏洞引向劣质修复方案；（2）判定翻转（verdict-flip），通过不可判定的可达性链使可利用性判定不稳定。防御方面提出CCD（因果反事实防御），采用留一法计算每个检索文档的因果影响，标记影响力集中于低信任文档的回答；同时提出聚合变体以应对自适应影响力分散攻击。

Result: 行动腐败攻击在五个测试模型（包括前沿推理模型）上每次运行均成功，覆盖两个合成领域和一个真实CVE（CVE-2021-33813）；判定翻转攻击随机成功，随模型能力提升而减少但不会消失。CCD在四个良性多文档对照组上零误报地定位了攻击者控制的文档，聚合变体可捕获自适应的影响力分散攻击者。

Conclusion: 攻击成功的根本原则是：当消除歧义的事实必须被"推断"而非"读取"时，RAG系统容易被组合性误导。这一洞见表明，安全副驾驶的防御不能仅依赖文档级内容审查，而需要审计文档间的组合对最终回答的因果影响，这正是CCD所提供的。

Abstract: Every document a security copilot retrieves can be true, instruction-free, and non-contradictory --- and the copilot can still be driven to assess a critical, exploitable vulnerability correctly and then recommend a remediation that leaves it open. We study this failure in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) backing analyst-facing copilots in Security Operations Centers, and identify a class of attacks, \emph{\compmis{}} (COMA), in which every adversarial document is factually correct, instruction-free, non-contradictory, and distributionally benign --- yet the answer is misled by their \emph{composition}. We realize \compmis{} through \emph{action-corruption}, which steers a correctly-diagnosed vulnerability toward an inferior remediation, and \emph{verdict-flip}, which destabilizes the exploitability verdict via an undecidable reachability chain. Action-corruption bites all five tested models --- including frontier reasoning models --- on every run, on two synthetic domains and a real CVE (CVE-2021-33813); verdict-flip bites stochastically, decreasing with model capability but never vanishing. A single principle governs both: the attack succeeds when the disambiguating fact must be \emph{inferred} rather than \emph{read}. We propose \ccd{} (Causal Counterfactual Defense), an audit that measures the leave-one-out causal influence of each retrieved document and flags answers whose influence concentrates on low-trust documents. \ccd{} localizes the attack to attacker-controlled documents with no false positives on four benign multi-document controls; an adaptive influence-spreading adversary is caught by an \emph{aggregate} variant. We release attack seeds and a \ccd{} reference implementation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [GxP-Agent: Process-DAG Topology for Reliable Clinical Trial Programming with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16890)
*Jaime Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GxP-Agent，一个基于有向无环图（DAG）编码监管流程的多智能体系统，用于CDISC标准下的临床试验编程任务。该系统将数据集生成分解为15个领域特定节点，通过工作智能体、验证门控和条件重试执行，在CDISC-Bench基准上实现了100%的结构匹配（49/49变量、254条记录），远超所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验编程（将研究方案转换为符合CDISC标准的分析就绪数据集）是监管提交中的瓶颈环节，但现有的LLM代码生成在此任务上完全失败——11次单次尝试中5个前沿模型均未能产出有效的受试者级分析数据集，说明单纯依赖LLM推理能力不足。

Method: GxP-Agent将监管过程顺序编码为有向无环图（DAG），将庞大的数据集生成任务分解为15个领域特定节点，由配备pharmaverse技能上下文的工作智能体执行，并集成验证门控和条件重试机制。系统在CDISC-Bench（基于FDA试点提交数据CDISCPilot01构建的执行型基准）上进行评估，包含254名受试者和49个ground-truth变量。

Result: GxP-Agent配合Claude Sonnet 4.6在三次独立运行中实现100%结构匹配（49/49变量、254条正确记录），而最佳检索增强基线仅达到59.2%，所有单智能体和扁平多智能体方法均为0%。DAG拓扑还使较弱模型受益：GPT-4.1在相同DAG下达到59.2%平均结构匹配，而在其他架构下均为0%。方法在ADAE（不良事件）任务上泛化良好，首次尝试即实现100%结构匹配（9节点分支DAG、55个变量、1,191条记录）。

Conclusion: 将领域过程知识编码为图拓扑结构——而非依赖LLM推理本身——是实现可靠、合规（GxP）的临床试验编程的关键使能因素，为LLM在监管级医疗软件任务中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Clinical trial programming -- transforming study protocols into analysis-ready datasets under CDISC standards -- is a bottleneck in regulatory submissions, yet LLM-based code generation fails catastrophically on this task: across 11 single-shot attempts with five frontier models, none produces a valid subject-level analysis dataset. We introduce GxP-Agent, a multi-agent system that encodes regulatory process ordering as a directed acyclic graph (DAG), decomposing monolithic dataset generation into 15 domain-specific nodes executed by worker agents with pharmaverse skill context, validation gates, and conditional retry. On CDISC-Bench, a new execution-based benchmark built from the FDA pilot submission CDISCPilot01 (254 subjects, 49 ground-truth ADSL variables), GxP-Agent with Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves 100% structural match (49/49 variables, 254 correct records) across three independent runs, compared to 59.2% for the best retrieval-augmented baseline and 0% for all single-agent and flat multi-agent approaches. The DAG topology also enables weaker models: GPT-4.1 achieves 59.2% mean structural match under the same DAG, where it scores 0% under every other architecture. The approach generalizes to ADAE (adverse events; 9-node branching DAG, 55 variables, 1,191 records), achieving 100% structural match on the first attempt. These results demonstrate that encoding domain process knowledge as graph topology -- rather than relying on LLM reasoning alone -- is a key enabler for reliable, GxP-compliant clinical trial programming.

</details>


### [89] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aegis是一个运行时治理系统，将Agentic AI模型的输出视为动作提案而非已授权动作，通过可信决策层在工具执行前进行调解。在评估语料中，该系统实现了零次被治理的冒险工具应用和零次被治理的冒险副作用完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统请求的工具动作可以修改文件、发送消息、启动作业或改变工作流状态，将安全问题从有害文本生成转移到有害操作副作用。提示级别的治理可以塑造模型行为，但无法创建执行边界，因此需要运行时治理系统来提供真正的安全保障。

Method: Aegis将模型输出视为动作提案，通过可信决策层在执行前进行调解。系统根据活动策略状态评估提案，在服务器端解析溯源信息，在不确定情况下安全失败，并将选定的案例路由到基于法定人数的非单边授权路径（Senate-style settlement）。评估使用重复沙箱语料库，涵盖五个运行族、42个任务、三个条件和每个族十次重复。

Result: 在6,300行提示策略条件数据中产生79条冒险比较器路径泄漏行；在2,100行Aegis治理数据中，记录了零次被治理的模拟工具应用和零次被治理的冒险副作用完成。所有1,832次Aegis尝试的治理行保留了受信任的Aegis解析溯源信息，所有1,019次Senate结算行具有法定人数和最终签名计数证据。

Conclusion: 结果不支持普遍自主Agent安全性的结论，但支持更局限的系统声明：在评估的沙箱语料库中，运行时动作边界治理阻止了观察到的冒险提案成为被治理的副作用。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [90] [The Price of Thinking: Reasoning Effort as a Model-Specific API Contract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16956)
*Yeabin Moon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过注册的配对对比实验，考察API合同中推理努力条款（显式高努力 vs 省略）对成本和准确率的影响。使用30道AIME 2026题目、每题5次调用，发现显式高努力合同每调用成本显著更高（+$0.01031），但未检测到准确率差异（+0.0133，置信区间允许最多+4.67个百分点），每正确答案成本在省略合同下更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: API买家购买的是包含日期和多种条款的合同（而非仅模型名称），其中推理努力条款（reasoning-effort term）的语义和影响尚不明确，尤其是同一模型下显式指定与省略该条款对成本和性能的影响需要严格的实验检验。

Method: 采用注册的前后配对对比设计：对Sonnet 5分别使用显式高努力和省略推理努力条款的合同，在30个AIME 2026题上各进行5次调用；每次付费尝试分配一个冻结的终态类别；推理阶段对题目重采样但保留重复调用；预先冻结请求注册表、解析器、终态分类法、统计方案和分析管线。

Result: 显式高努力合同比省略合同每调用平均成本高$0.01031（置信区间[+$0.00204, +$0.01974]）；准确率对比为+0.0133（区间[-0.0267, +0.0467]），未检测到显著差异但无法排除最多4.67个百分点的增益；每正确答案成本：高努力合同$0.08665，省略合同$0.07662。额外的模型API元数据和原始响应探针揭示了模型特定的省略语义差异。

Conclusion: 在同一模型下，显式指定高推理努力会显著增加调用成本，但未检测到相应的准确率提升；省略推理努力条款在成本效益上更优。研究结论严格限定于所测试的模型、任务和收集日期，且省略条款的语义因模型（甚至同一提供商内）而异。

Abstract: API buyers purchase a dated contract, not a model name alone: the contract includes the requested and served model, reasoning-effort term or its omission, output rail, service product, prompt, and price schedule. We study the reasoning-effort term through a registered paired contrast of Sonnet 5 with explicit high effort against the same model with effort omitted, using 30 AIME 2026 items and five calls per item. Every paid attempt was assigned one frozen terminal category, and inference resampled items while retaining their repeated calls. Mean delivered cost was \$0.01031 per call higher under the explicit-high contract than under the omitted contract [+\$0.00204, +\$0.01974]. The corresponding accuracy contrast was +0.0133 [-0.0267, +0.0467]; we did not detect an accuracy difference, and the interval permits a gain of up to 4.67 percentage points that this design cannot rule out. Cost per correct answer was \$0.08665 under the high-effort contract and \$0.07662 under the omitted contract, as registered point estimates. A dated contract census, Models-API metadata, and preregistered raw-response probes further documented model-specific omission semantics, including within a provider; claims remained at documentation grade when raw structure was indeterminate. The request registry, parser, terminal taxonomy, statistical plan, and analysis pipeline were frozen before outcomes were examined; the resulting claims are bounded to the model, task, and collection date studied.

</details>


### [91] [FedPref: Federated Preference Learning for Structured Radiology Report Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16971)
*Flint Xiaofeng Fan,Cheston Tan,Yew-Soon Ong,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedPref，一种联邦偏好学习框架，通过冻结公共语言模型生成候选JSON提取、本地标注排序、协同训练Qwen3-8B适配器，实现多中心放射科报告信息抽取，在数据不均且不可共享的场景下提升F1性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科报告以自由文本描述发现和位置，但下游检索与分析需要固定模式的关系（即结构化抽取）。不同机构的数据分布不均：小医院本地证据少，而直接汇集数据可能不可行。因此需要一种在不共享报告或标注前提下，让数据量不均的机构协同获益的方案。

Method: FedPref框架包含三部分：(1) 冻结的公共语言模型作为异构教师池，为每个样本提出替代JSON提取候选；(2) 本地标注对候选进行偏好排序；(3) 各站点仅共享模型更新，协同训练紧凑的Qwen3-8B适配器。异构教师池提供跨模型对比，避免单模型重复采样导致的坍缩问题。

Result: 在六个模拟医院（数据量和疾病患病率不均）的开发数据上，FedPref相比各站点独立训练，客户端平均F1提升2.49点，最差站点F1提升9.10点，最大增益出现在数据最少的站点。集中式训练在池化偏好对联合上的客户端平均F1比FedPref高2.66点。在锁定的400份报告人工验证黄金测试集上，FedPref达到68.68 F1，集中训练为71.67，排序保持一致。

Conclusion: FedPref使数据量不均、无法汇集数据的机构能够在不共享报告或标注的前提下，通过协同训练获益，尤其对数据最少的站点增益最大。

Abstract: Radiology reports describe findings and locations in free text, but downstream search and analysis require these relations in a fixed schema. Learning this extraction requires labels that are unevenly distributed across institutions: smaller hospitals have less local evidence, and pooling data may be infeasible. We introduce FedPref: frozen public language models propose alternative JSON extractions, local annotations rank them, and sites collaboratively train compact Qwen3-8B adapters while sharing only model updates. A heterogeneous teacher pool provides cross-model contrast when repeated single-model samples collapse. On development data from six simulated hospitals with unequal data volume and disease prevalence, FedPref improves client-mean F1 by 2.49 points and worst-site F1 by 9.10 points compared with training each site in isolation, with the largest gains at the sites holding the least data. Central training on the pooled preference-pair union is 2.66 points higher on client-mean F1. On a locked, 400-report manually validated gold test set, FedPref reaches 68.68 F1 and pooled training 71.67, preserving that same ordering. FedPref thus lets institutions with unequal, unpooled data benefit from collaboration without ever sharing reports or annotations.

</details>


### [92] [The Problem Is the Problem: Towards Scalable Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16977)
*Zeyu Zheng,Shengtong Zhang,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的人机协作数学发现范式FAR（Find, Attempt, and Recommend），将人类从预先选定单个问题改为指定研究方向，系统在文献语料库中自动搜索候选猜想/开放问题，经多级筛选后将值得人工评审的成果聚焦出来。在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中筛选出77项供团队评审的成果，发现了多个重要问题的新结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助数学发现工作流中，人力集中在问题的前期选择和后期评审两个阶段，这两端正在成为研究级数学的瓶颈。前沿模型的推理能力和专家评审都是稀缺资源，需要更好地分配这些资源以提高AI辅助数学发现的效率。

Method: 提出FAR级联流程（文献到评审）：首先在研究者指定的研究方向上从大规模文献语料中搜索候选问题（从5,245篇组合学论文中恢复6,453个候选猜想/开放问题）；然后经过多级过滤（4,717个貌似良定且仍然开放的问题）；再进行推理和自动化分流（筛出598个潜在解法）；最终选择77项交由作者团队评审，形成"文献→尝试→推荐"的级联体系。

Result: 在组合学试点中，从5,245篇论文中提取6,453个候选猜想，过滤到4,717个开放问题，经推理和分流后筛出598个潜在解法，最终77项进入人工评审，发现了关于Davies–Jenssen–Perkins–Roberts、Erdős–Straus、Ikenmeyer–Pak–Panova、Lund–Saraf–Wolf等猜想/问题的有趣新结果。

Conclusion: 实验证明了这种"人指定方向、系统搜索并过滤问题、人聚焦评审筛选产物"的新人机协作模式对数学发现的有效性，未来有望扩展AI辅助数学研究的规模与效率。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of contributing to mathematical research. In research practice, frontier-model reasoning is a limited resource, and expert mathematical review is even more sharply constrained. Allocating these scarce resources well is therefore central to making AI-assisted mathematical discovery efficient. In most current AI-for-math workflows, human effort is concentrated at the beginning and end, in selecting suitable research problems and later reviewing the resulting artifacts. These two stages are becoming bottlenecks for research-level mathematics. We address them by proposing a new human-AI discovery paradigm. The human input is no longer a single problem selected in advance, but a research direction in which the experts have interest and expertise. The system then searches a broad literature corpus for candidate problems in that direction. Inspired by search and recommender systems, we build Find, Attempt, and Recommend (FAR), a literature-to-review cascade that automates the search for suitable problems and focuses human attention on artifacts that have passed several stages of filtering. In a combinatorics pilot, the pipeline starts from 5,245 combinatorics papers, recovers 6,453 candidate conjectures or open problems, and filters them to 4,717 apparently well-posed and still-open conjectures. Subsequent reasoning and automated triage stages surface 598 potential resolutions and select 77 items for author-team review. Among them, we identify many interesting discoveries, including results on conjectures and questions of Davies--Jenssen--Perkins--Roberts, Erdős--Straus, Ikenmeyer--Pak--Panova, and Lund--Saraf--Wolf. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of this new mode of human-AI collaboration for mathematical discovery.

</details>


### [93] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillEffect是一个"检查降级"（checked-lowering）运行时系统，用于在Agent工具调用时强制执行资源/内存边界，通过独立的检查器在授权执行前验证程序降级是否正确，并有可审计的有界实现和注册的输出后置条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型将Agent技能（Agent Skills）实例化为具体程序时，即使语义正确，程序也可能一次性加载整个输入，超出单个工具调用的内存限制，导致执行失败。

Method: SkillEffect包含三个核心要素：可恢复的源关系（recoverable source relation）、可审计的有界实现（audited bounded implementation）、以及注册的输出后置条件（registered output postcondition）。在授予执行权限之前，独立检查器根据提交的程序和不可变输入重建每个提议的降级。每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数和后置条件；而公共运行时统一提供检查选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段发布。六个插件在五种执行模式下实例化同一契约（从流式归约到有界堆Top-k）。通用性是架构性的而非自动的：每个受支持的计算需要审计的关系插件，而分发、资源控制、执行和发布机制在插件间共享。

Result: 在六个算子族中，有界访问显著降低了峰值内存并提高了在外部固定容量下的完成率。XLSX接入研究和Top-k扩展表明，新关系和新保留状态模式可复用同一信任边界；检查器接受所有评估的合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性提议。

Conclusion: 一个检查降级架构可以在Agent工具分发时强制实施异构的注册内存关系，从而有效解决语言模型生成程序时的内存过载问题。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [94] [Memory Is Communication: The Frontier Between Remembering and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17053)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨有限理性智能体在同时受限于记忆与通信预算时，如何在利用自身历史信息与同伴消息之间分配信息预算的问题。作者提出了"记忆-信号前沿"概念，用于刻画在给定任务与决策规则下，达到特定性能阈值所需的记忆与消息速率组合形成的可达区域。基于初步参照博弈实验，作者假设当历史信息能带来更大损失降低时，智能体需要更少的同伴通信，并提出了通过控制记忆与消息速率来估计前沿并验证该预测的实验方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限理性智能体在决策时可以从自身历史记忆和同伴消息两个渠道获取信息，但两者都受资源限制。现有研究缺乏一个统一框架来刻画在记忆与通信双重预算约束下，智能体应如何最优分配信息预算的问题。

Method: 作者定义了"可达区域"（achievable region）来描述在给定任务和决策规则下，记忆速率与消息速率组合所能达到的性能阈值边界，并将该边界称为"记忆-信号前沿"（remembering-signaling frontier）。通过初步参照博弈实验，考察了目标重复度与隐藏周期性规则对消息长度的影响，并提出通过操纵记忆与消息速率来估计前沿的实验方法。

Result: 初步实验显示，目标重复出现时伴随更短的成功消息，而隐藏循环规则带来的可预测性并未缩短消息长度。这表明历史信息带来的损失降低程度可能确实影响所需的通信量。

Conclusion: 作者提出"记忆-信号前沿"作为研究记忆与通信资源分配问题的理论框架，并给出了实验验证方案。核心假设是：同等历史效益下，历史带来的损失降低越多，智能体所需的同伴通信越少。

Abstract: A bounded agent may obtain information for a decision from its own past, from peers, or from both sources. Retaining task-relevant history can reduce later communication, while a peer message can supply what memory lacks. Under limits on both resources, how should an agent allocate its information budget? Given a fixed task and decision rule, the memory and message rate pairs attaining a performance threshold form an achievable region under specified rules for using history and peer observations. We call its efficient boundary the remembering--signaling frontier. Across conditions where history permits the same maximum reduction in task loss, we hypothesize that a bounded agent will need less peer communication when it obtains a larger loss reduction from history. In preliminary referential games, target repetition coincided with shorter successful messages, while predictability from a hidden cyclic rule did not shorten them. Experiments varying memory and message rates can estimate the frontier and test this prediction across cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [95] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSCO，一个零样本、严格黑盒的防御框架，通过在提示词层面进行分布引导的后缀扩展来抵御文本到图像生成模型中的NSFW内容生成，无需访问模型内部或重新训练，在两个评估集上分别将攻击成功率（ASR）降低37.7%和25.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像模型的NSFW防御大多基于白盒假设（如文本编码器优化、权重编辑或推理时干预），无法扩展到专有模型；而基于LLM提示词重写的黑盒替代方案在"良性对抗"问题（prompt在语言学上安全但仍触发有害生成）上效果有限，因此需要一种严格黑盒且能处理良性对抗场景的防御方法。

Method: DiSCO是一种纯提示词级别的即插即用黑盒防御模块，核心包含三个环节：(1) 分布引导的后缀扩展，利用波束搜索在输入提示词上扩展安全后缀；(2) 对比评分，基于目标模型自身生成的"安全图像池"和"不安全图像池"进行排序优选；(3) 迭代自适应反馈机制，持续输出直到生成安全内容，全程无需访问模型内部参数。

Result: 在I2P基准上，DiSCO对无防御模型和有防御模型在多种红队攻击下分别实现37.7%和25.13%的攻击成功率（ASR）降低，同时保持语义保真度并提升图像一致性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为一种黑盒、架构无关的模块，可以即插即用地部署到任何文本到图像系统而无需改动模型本身，为专有/闭源模型的NSFW安全防护提供了可行方案，填补了白盒防御无法覆盖的空白。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [96] [KernelArc: A Multi-Agent Framework for GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17071)
*Joyjit Kundu,Ben Stoffelen,Kaili Wang,Peter Vrancx,Ludovic Denoyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KernelArc是一个多智能体框架，用于异构工作负载上的自主GPU内核优化。策略专业化智能体并行运行，通过仅结论的共享内存、确定性基准守护和带平台触发的只读跨智能体状态进行协调。在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上，使用SOL-ExecBench代表性工作负载进行评估，生成的实现涵盖自定义BF16 GEMM、静态cuBLASLt Expert-API配置表、融合MoE反向、形状门控解码器层融合、原生NVFP4分组查询注意力和分页预填充注意力，在公共SOL-ExecBench排行榜（2026年7月30日快照）上于代表性L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核优化面临异构工作负载带来的巨大搜索空间，现有方法缺乏系统化的多智能体协调机制来扩大探索范围并在固定候选预算内找到更强的解。

Method: 提出KernelArc多智能体框架：(1)策略专业化智能体并行运行；(2)仅结论共享内存实现智能体间通信；(3)确定性基准守护保证评估可靠；(4)只读跨智能体状态配合平台触发草稿机制。在NVIDIA H100和B200 GPU上对SOL-ExecBench类别代表性工作负载进行评估。

Result: 生成的实现覆盖六类内核优化（BF16 GEMM、cuBLASLt Expert-API配置、融合MoE反向、形状门控解码器融合、NVFP4 GQA、分页预填充注意力），在SOL-ExecBench排行榜快照中的L1、L2、Quantization和FlashInfer任务上排名第一。

Conclusion: 共享多智能体搜索能拓宽探索范围并在固定候选预算内达到更强解；各协调特征的价值取决于具体内核和优化阶段，没有一种特征在所有场景下普遍最优。

Abstract: We present KernelArc, a multi-agent framework for autonomous GPU kernel optimization across heterogeneous workloads. Strategy-specialized agents run in parallel and coordinate through conclusions-only shared memory, a deterministic benchmark guard, and read-only cross-agent state with plateau-triggered drafting. We evaluate \kernelarc{} on NVIDIA H100 and B200 GPUs using category-representative SOL-ExecBench workloads. The resulting implementations span custom BF16 GEMM, static cuBLASLt Expert-API configuration tables, fused mixture-of-experts backward, shape-gated decoder-layer fusion, native NVFP4 grouped-query attention, and paged prefill attention. At the public SOL-ExecBench leaderboard snapshot recorded on July~30, 2026, these submissions ranked first on representative L1, L2, Quantization, and FlashInfer tasks. The trajectories support the paper's central motivation: shared multi-agent search can broaden exploration and reach stronger incumbents within a fixed candidate budget, while the value of individual coordination features depends on the kernel and optimization stage.

</details>


### [97] [A decodability criterion predicts when hidden-state selection beats majority voting in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17124)
*Zhixiang wang,Ziliang Hong,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CASE（Correctness-Axis SElection），一种基于答案token隐藏状态训练线性门控来选择高置信度候选答案的动态选择组合器，用于替代多数投票。核心贡献是提出了"可解码性"（decodability）度量，该度量无泄漏地衡量门控对正确/错误候选的排序能力，可预测隐藏状态选择是否优于投票（Pearson r=0.75），为模型和任务选择合适的选择策略提供预先可测的实用判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票在困难问题上不可靠，因为采样答案间存在相关错误，错误答案可能获胜且增加采样反而恶化决策。从模型隐藏状态读取正确性信号是替代方案，但其准确率在不同模型和任务间差异大，且缺乏衡量其可信度的指标。

Method: CASE在答案token的隐藏状态上训练一个线性门控，对每个采样候选打分并选择得分最高者。论文提出"可解码性"（decodability）这一无泄漏度量，评估门控对同一问题正确候选与错误候选的排序能力；同时揭示了传统探针在问题分组评估下存在的"问题身份泄漏"（question-identity leakage）问题。

Result: 在留出数据上，decodability预测选择相对投票的准确率增益，Pearson相关系数r=0.75，决策阈值接近AUC=0.60。在通用和医学LLM上，CASE相比投票在中难度问题上提升最多19分，困难问题上提升16.8分。Decodability取决于模型需回忆的对齐知识而非模型规模，其对未见科学领域的预测迁移误差在3.8分以内。

Conclusion: Decodability提供了实用判据，可在给定模型和任务上预先测量，用于决定选择学习式选择（CASE）还是多数投票，兼顾了性能提升与可靠性保证。

Abstract: Combining the answers a large language model (LLM) samples for a question into one decision is a test-time information fusion problem, usually solved by majority voting. Voting is unreliable on difficult questions, where the sampled answers share correlated errors, so the wrong answer can win and drawing more samples makes the decision worse. Selecting a candidate by reading a correctness signal from the model's hidden states is a promising alternative, but its accuracy varies across models and tasks, and no measure indicates when it can be trusted. In this paper, we propose CASE (Correctness-Axis SElection), a dynamic selection combiner that trains a linear gate on the answer-token hidden state and selects the highest-scoring candidate. Its main contribution is decodability, a leakage-free measure of how well the gate ranks a question's correct candidates above its incorrect ones, which predicts whether hidden-state selection will outperform voting. A conventional probe appears accurate only because of question-identity leakage, which vanishes under question-grouped evaluation. On held-out data, decodability predicts the accuracy gain of selection over voting with a Pearson correlation r=0.75 and a decision threshold near AUC=0.60. Across general and medical LLMs, CASE improves over voting by up to 19 points on medium-difficulty questions and 16.8 points on hard questions. Decodability depends on the aligned knowledge a model must recall, not on its scale, and its prediction transfers to an unseen scientific domain within 3.8 points. It thus provides a practical criterion, measurable in advance for a given model and task, for choosing between learned selection and majority voting.

</details>


### [98] [Toward Personal Intelligence Through Cooperative Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17128)
*Yashar Talebirad,Osman Jime,Ali Parsaee,Eden Redman,Yongbin Kim,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"合作式观察"（cooperative observation）框架，认为个人AI系统对用户目标的建模质量受限于其可观察到的信息，而用户通过评估系统行为来维持、调整或撤销系统的观察权限，形成良性或恶性的反馈循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人AI系统需要建模用户的目标、约束和持续承诺，但建模质量受制于系统能观察到的信息范围。仅仅扩大观察范围并不能自动改善协助质量，因为有界系统必须选择和压缩信息。作者提出观察瓶颈具有合作性结构，值得作为个人智能研究的框架来探讨。

Method: 提出"合作式观察"概念——系统构建用户生活的部分模型，用户评估其行为，用户的同意与控制决定系统未来可观察的内容，形成反馈循环。作者还报告了Organizm原型工具在六个月中用于单个受试者的初步定性研究，并提出了评估观察质量如何影响个人AI的方向。

Result: 提供了一个关于个人AI观察瓶颈的合作性框架，以及一个六个月的Organizm单受试者初步说明性报告，并给出了若干评估方向。

Conclusion: 合作式观察可作为个人智能领域的构建性框架，连接了系统的有用性、用户信任与未来的数据访问权限，对于设计和评估个人AI系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: A personal AI system needs a model of the user's goals, constraints, and ongoing commitments to plan and act on their behalf, and the quality of that model is bounded by what the system can observe. Broader observation does not by itself improve assistance because a bounded system must select and compress information for the task at hand. We argue that this observation bottleneck has a cooperative structure: the system builds a partial model of the user's changing life, the user evaluates its actions, and the user's consent and control shape what it can observe next. Useful and inspectable behavior can give users a reason to maintain or expand the observation channel, while failures can lead them to correct, narrow, revoke, or abandon it. We use the term cooperative observation for this feedback loop among usefulness, trust, and future access, and propose it as a framework for personal intelligence. We report a preliminary single-subject account from Organizm, a prototype used over six months, and outline evaluation directions for measuring how observation quality shapes personal AI.

</details>


### [99] [KnowSim: Evaluating Information Calibration in LLM Assistants with User Simulators that Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17150)
*Yoonjoo Lee,Hyoungwook Jin,Tae Soo Kim,Shaoyang Zhang,Philippe Laban,Q. Vera Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KNOWSIM，一个基于显式知识状态建模的用户模拟器评估框架，用于评估大语言模型在知识密集型任务中的信息校准能力。该框架以带前置关系的信息单元图表示用户知识状态，并依据学习理论中的更新规则演化，通过知识增益、传递校准和认知过载三个指标度量信息校准的机制性表现。在705个人机交互会话上验证，其排序与人类判断达到73-74%的符号一致性，优于三个基线模拟器；应用于9个LLM的评估揭示了不同用户知识水平下最优模型会发生变化，暴露了标准评估无法捕捉的能力倾向交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于评估和训练LLM的用户模拟器未显式建模用户知识，因此既无法在不同知识水平下产生逼真的交互，也无法反映随用户知识演变交互如何展开。这导致LLM在知识密集型任务中与用户有效协作时的信息校准能力（将内容匹配到用户不断变化的理解和认知负荷）难以被准确评估。

Method: KNOWSIM框架包含：(1) 一个维护显式知识状态的用户模拟器，知识状态表示为具有前置关系的信息单元图；(2) 基于学习理论推导的更新规则来演化知识状态；(3) 直接从知识状态轨迹计算三个指标——知识增益、传递校准和认知过载，分别刻画信息校准的关键机制方面。

Result: 在跨越两个领域、按知识水平分层的705个人机交互会话上验证，KNOWSIM的排序与人类判断显著对齐（73-74%符号一致性），优于三个基线模拟器。应用至9个LLM的评估表明，最优模型随用户知识水平而变化，揭示了标准评估中不可见的能力倾向交互效应。

Conclusion: KNOWSIM通过显式建模用户知识状态及其演化，提供了比传统用户模拟器更准确、更具机制可解释性的LLM信息校准评估手段，并揭示了模型性能与用户知识水平之间存在交互作用，为个性化AI助手的设计与评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: To effectively collaborate with users on knowledge-intensive tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) must perform information calibration: matching content to a user's evolving understanding and cognitive capacity. Yet user simulators used to evaluate and train LLMs do not explicitly model user knowledge so they neither produce realistic interactions across knowledge levels nor reflect how interactions unfold as that knowledge evolves. To close this gap, we introduce KNOWSIM, an evaluation framework built around a user simulator that maintains explicit knowledge states, represented as a graph of Information Units with prerequisite relationships, that evolve under update rules grounded in learning theory. KNOWSIM computes three metrics (Knowledge Gain, Delivery Calibration, Cognitive Overload) directly from the knowledge state trajectory, reflecting key mechanistic aspects of information calibration. We validate KNOWSIM against 705 human-AI sessions across two domains, stratified by knowledge level: its rankings align significantly with human judgments (73-74% sign agreement), outperforming three baseline simulators. Applied to 9 LLMs, KNOWSIM reveals that the best model shifts by user knowledge level, revealing aptitude-treatment interactions invisible to standard evaluation.

</details>


### [100] [Synthesizing Feature Extractors: An Agentic Approach for Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17170)
*Hai Xia,Carlos Ansótegui,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）在智能体check-fix-verify循环中自动合成Python特征提取器的方法，用于约束满足问题（CSP）的算法选择。该方法生成的解释性、问题特定的特征提取器在三个组合问题上的算法选择准确率持续优于专家手工设计的mzn2feat特征和基于Transformer的trans2feat变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束满足问题的算法选择依赖于能够捕获问题结构的特征提取，但手工设计特征提取器需要深入的领域专业知识，且当新问题类别出现时迅速成为瓶颈。因此需要一种自动化方法来生成特征提取器。

Method: 利用LLM作为智能体，在check-fix-verify循环中自动合成可执行的Python脚本作为特征提取器。给定高层MiniZinc模型和实例，LLM智能体生成代码来构建类型化图表示，并计算图密度、变量聚类、约束紧密度等结构属性。

Result: 在车辆路线规划、汽车排序、固定长度纠错码三个组合问题上，使用五种最先进求解器的组合进行评测。合成特征提取器得到的算法选择器在测试集准确率上持续超越专家手工设计的mzn2feat特征（在FLECC上最高提升8.3个百分点）以及最佳Transformer-based trans2feat变体。

Conclusion: 合成的特征提取器不仅在性能上优于专家设计和深度学习方法，同时保持了可检查性（inspectable），为CSP算法选择中的特征工程提供了自动化且可解释的替代方案。

Abstract: Algorithm selection for constraint satisfaction problems requires extracting features that capture problem structure. Manually designing feature extractors demands deep domain expertise and quickly becomes a bottleneck when new problem classes appear. We present an automated approach that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) in an agentic check--fix--verify loop to synthesize executable Python scripts that act as interpretable, problem-specific feature extractors. Given a high-level MiniZinc model and an instance, the LLM agent generates code that constructs a typed graph representation and computes structural properties such as graph density, variable clustering, and constraint tightness. We evaluate our approach on three combinatorial problems (vehicle routing, car sequencing, fixed-length error-correcting codes) with a portfolio of five state-of-the-art solvers. The synthesized extractors yield algorithm selectors that consistently outperform both expert-curated mzn2feat features (up to $8.3$ percentage points (pp) test-set accuracy on FLECC) and the best transformer-based trans2feat variants. In the meanwhile, the synthesized feature extractors remain inspectable.

</details>


### [101] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了五个广泛使用的安全基准套件在26个开源小型语言模型（SLM）上的有效性和鲁棒性，发现在统一评判标准下，模糊性判断占据主导地位，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关。研究揭示现有以LLM为中心的安全基准不能作为SLM安全评估的独立证据，并指出存在能力-安全混杂问题，使得排行榜排名在合理的模糊性处理下会显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLM）越来越多地部署在资源受限、隐私敏感的场景中，其安全性和偏见失败可能引发安全和社\r会风险。然而现有的AI安全/安全/合规基准是为大型语言模型设计的，可能无法可靠迁移到SLM上。因此需要回答：这些基准能否有效且可靠地评估SLM？

Method: 作者对五个广泛使用的基准套件在26个开源SLM上进行大规模评估，采用统一评判标准（对有害、安全或模糊/不相关响应分别赋予0、1、0.5分），系统分析模糊判断的比例及其与提示复杂度、模型架构、词汇密度、输出困惑度、输出长度等特征的关系，并检验聚合平均分排行榜在合理模糊性处理下的稳定性。

Result: 研究发现模糊性判断在各基准中占据主导地位，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关。模糊率随词汇密度、输出困惑度和输出长度增加而增加，随词汇复杂程度、自相干性和回复-提示相似度增加而降低。这揭示了能力-安全混杂效应。由于模糊性普遍存在，基于聚合平均分的排行榜在数学上是脆弱的：即使底层输出不变，模型排名在合理的模糊性处理下也会显著改变。

Conclusion: 以LLM为中心的安全基准不足以作为SLM安全评估的独立证据。当前基准在SLM评估中存在能力与安全的混淆问题，且排行榜结果对模糊性处理方式高度敏感，需要为SLM开发专门的安全评估方法。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [102] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [103] [Explicit State Elicitation Is Not Enough: A Controlled Audit of Memory-Policy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17247)
*Yihang Chen,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Huan Xu,Shuaiting Li,Yiqi Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套针对个性化智能体内存管理的实证审计协议，通过结构化中间输出逐步排查数据捷径、混淆的提示变更、标签关联性、分解语义证据及供应商级执行失败。研究发现初始的480例开发集增益主要来自词汇分裂而非真正能力，在控制反事实集上暴露状态定义可提升准确率，但显式状态输出字段的增量收益不显著，且示例级准确率高估了反事实一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需要决定检索到的用户记忆是应被使用、忽略、更新还是查询，这直接影响当前任务。然而，现有的评估方法可能因数据捷径和提示词变更混淆而产生虚假的性能提升，缺乏系统性的审计方法来验证中间输出的真实有效性。

Method: 提出四步审计协议：(1)审计数据集捷径（如TF-IDF词汇诊断）；(2)隔离捆绑的提示变更；(3)检查中间标签是否与答案关联（标签条件化诊断）；(4)测试分解语义证据并审计供应商级执行失败。构建480例合成开发集和160例冻结控制反事实集（40个匹配的四路家庭，规则推导参考策略），对比Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-OSS-120B。

Result: 480例开发集显示状态结构化提示捆绑带来大增益，但TF-IDF显示词汇可分离且无正面的独立Ignore案例。在控制反事实集上，暴露四种状态定义提升准确率，但显式状态输出字段对Llama-3.3-70B无显著增益，对GPT-OSS-120B仅边际非显著增益。标签条件化诊断显示状态标签可确定性映射到策略，属于标签关联而非内部机制证据。完整四路家庭成功率罕见，GPT-OSS的分解语义证据条件因供应商验证失败而不可用。

Conclusion: 审计方法有效揭示了数据捷径和标签条件化伪信号；示例级准确率会高估反事实一致性，需采用家庭级和种子稳定性分析。研究仅评估策略分类而非下游响应、工具动作或记忆库变更，提示未来工作需拓展至完整下游评估链路。

Abstract: Personalized agents must decide whether retrieved user memory should be used, ignored, updated, or queried before it affects a current task. We use this setting to develop an empirical audit protocol for structured intermediate outputs: first audit dataset shortcuts, then isolate bundled prompt changes, check whether intermediate labels are answer-associated, test decomposed semantic evidence, and audit provider-level execution failures. A 480-example synthetic development set initially suggested large gains from a state-structured prompt bundle, but TF-IDF diagnostics showed lexical separability and no positive standalone Ignore cases. We therefore construct a frozen 160-example controlled counterfactual set with 40 matched four-way families and rule-derived reference policies. On this set, exposing the four state definitions improves accuracy, but an isolated explicit state-output field does not significantly improve policy accuracy for Llama-3.3-70B and gives only a marginal, non-significant gain for GPT-OSS-120B. Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [104] [Do LLMs Know a Good Hypothesis When They See One? Logit-Based Energy Scoring Outperforms Prompted LLM-as-Judge for Scientific Hypothesis Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17270)
*Swati Rajwal,Sanjay Das,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于logit能量的评分方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度而非比较判断来评估科学假设。在1,323篇论文、12个学科、7个语言模型的基准测试中，内在评分达到33.0%的Hit@1（对比提示式列表排序的16.6%），最强配置（10亿参数模型）达到53.1%，展示了内在模型置信度在科学假设评估中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地用于科学假设生成，但评估生成假设的质量仍是可信AI科学工作流的挑战。现有方法常使用LLM作为评判者或依赖语义相似度，这些方法可能偏向熟悉的观点而非新颖的想法，缺乏对假设内在质量和置信度的有效评估。

Method: 提出基于logit的energy scoring方法，利用语言模型的内在置信度来评估假设。在1,323篇论文（覆盖12个学科）上进行基准测试，将每篇论文与其假设及15个错误替代选项配对，比较内在评分与提示式列表排序两种方法在7个语言模型上的表现。

Result: 内在评分在两种评分器上的Hit@1达到33.0%，显著优于提示式列表排序的16.6%。最强的配置（10亿参数模型使用logit-based energy scoring）达到53.1%，但这是14种模型-评分器组合中选择的最大值。

Conclusion: 内在模型置信度在科学假设评估中展现出潜力，超越了基于比较判断的方法。该研究为基于置信度的可信AI科学发现方法提供了动机，但需要注意后验选择偏差（post hoc selection）对结果解读的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific hypothesis generation. However, evaluating generated hypotheses remains a challenge for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific workflows. Existing approaches often use LLMs as judges or rely on semantic similarity, which can favor familiar ideas over novel ones. We propose a logit-based energy scoring method that evaluates hypotheses using a language model's intrinsic confidence rather than comparative judgment. We benchmarked seven language models on 1,323 papers across 12 disciplines. Each paper was paired with its hypothesis and fifteen incorrect alternatives. Intrinsic scoring reached 33.0% Hit@1 pooled across both scorers, compared with 16.6% for prompted listwise ranking. The strongest configuration, a 1-billion-parameter model using logit-based energy scoring, reached 53.1%, though this was the maximum across 14 model-by-scorer combinations selected post hoc. Overall, intrinsic model confidence shows potential for scientific hypothesis evaluation. This study also motivates future research on confidence-based methods for trustworthy AI-enabled scientific discovery.

</details>


### [105] [ASI-Bench: At the Dawn of Artificial Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17271)
*Junwei Zhou,Zhen Sun,Binyu Li,Jiangyu Zhou,Yuexi Pan,Hengyu Wang,Honghe Ren,Xiaohan Jia,Xueyang Zhou,Xiaoyu Cao,Yongchao Chen,Yuanning Feng,Junhao Wu,Cheng Zhang,Sijia Chen,Haoyu Xue,Chengsong You,Huan Wang,Koutian Wu,Peigan Gao,Jiakun Wu,Wenzhe Li,Ergan Shang,Qingyuan Zheng,Jingjing Zhou,Ruixuan Jia,Yan Xu,Hongrui Zhang,Xiao-Han Ma,Zhengxiang Cheng,Yuexing Hao,Liting Mai,Xianglin Ji,Wenjun Zhang,Zhuofan Chen,Yixiao Huang,Chi Wang,Wenyue Hua,Yilun Hao,Yuantao Zhai,Ziyan Zhao,Jingyan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ASI-Bench，这是首个在通用研究领域联合评估AI系统创新探索和自主科学执行能力的基准，也是首个在同一研究项目内逐步撤回人类方法论指导以测试AI自主能力的基准。该基准包含11个科学领域的60个项目级研究任务，构建成本超过31,000人工小时。实验发现，在18种先进的智能体-模型配置下，平均得分从提供完整方法论指导时的50.91骤降至仅指定方法时的29.10，再到必须自主决定方法时的26.62，表明当前AI系统仍高度依赖人类指导，远未达到自主进行端到端项目级科学研究的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能超级智能（ASI）要求AI超越掌握现有知识，向探索未知、创造新知识和将新想法转化为可验证结果的方向发展。然而，当前AI系统的能力仍主要建立在学习、压缩和应用已有人类知识的基础上，现有基准也主要测试AI能否基于已有知识给出正确答案或在广泛人类指导下完成任务。因此，需要一个能系统性评估AI创新探索和自主科研执行能力的基准。

Method: ASI-Bench由40余位专家耗时31,000+人工小时构建，包含跨越11个科学领域的60个项目级研究任务。基准通过渐进式撤回方法论指导（从完整指导→仅指定方法→完全自主决定方法）来测试AI的自主科研能力。所有任务经过专家评审、AI辅助审计、沙盒执行和评分者验证等多重质量控制。

Result: 在18种最先进的智能体-模型配置上，平均得分从完整方法论指导下的50.91，降至仅指定方法时的29.10，再降至必须自主决定方法时的26.62。这一显著下降表明当前AI系统严重依赖人类指导，自主完成端到端项目级科学研究的能力仍然不足。

Conclusion: ASI-Bench揭示了当前AI系统在自主科学研究方面与ASI之间的巨大差距，证明了现有系统仍高度依赖人类方法论指导。该基准已向全球开放，邀请研究人员和开发者贡献新任务、挑战AI极限，以加速通往人工超级智能的进程。

Abstract: Artificial superintelligence (ASI) requires AI to move beyond mastering existing knowledge toward exploring the unknown, creating new knowledge, and turning new ideas into verifiable results. However, the capabilities of today's AI systems are still largely built on learning, compressing, and applying existing human knowledge. Accordingly, existing benchmarks primarily test whether AI can produce correct answers based on learned knowledge, or whether it can complete tasks under extensive human guidance. We therefore introduce ASI-Bench, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate AI systems' capabilities of innovative exploration and autonomous scientific execution across general research domains, and the first to progressively withdraw human methodological guidance within the same research project to test how far AI can proceed on its own. Built by over 40 experts with the cost of 31,000+ human hours, ASI-Bench contains 60 project-level research tasks across 11 scientific domains and progressively reduces methodological guidance to test whether AI can independently select methods, conduct research, and produce verifiable results. All tasks undergo expert review, AI-assisted auditing, sandbox execution, and scorer validation. Across 18 state-of-the-art agent--model configurations, the average score drops from 50.91 with full methodological guidance to 29.10 with only the method specified and 26.62 when agents must determine the method themselves. This sharp decline shows that current systems remain heavily dependent on human guidance and are still far from autonomously conducting end-to-end, project-level scientific research. ASI-Bench is open to the world. We invite researchers and builders everywhere to contribute new tasks, challenge the limits of today's AI, and help accelerate humanity's collective path toward artificial superintelligence at https://asibench.apexin.ai/submit.

</details>


### [106] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeAR（去中心化智能体推理）框架，将智能体推理从传统的中心化协议转变为自主的点对点协作模式，通过去中心化能力接地、思维图导航和拓扑更新三种机制，在9个多模态推理和文本QA基准上超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统依赖中心化协议，存在路由瓶颈和静态角色分配问题，在处理复杂多模态查询时效果不佳。作者希望通过去中心化架构解决这些问题。

Method: DeAR框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 去中心化能力接地，实现基于查询的智能体特化；(2) 思维图导航，用于目标化智能体间交互；(3) 拓扑更新，实现自适应错误修正。整个框架从中心控制转向自主对等协作。

Result: 在9个多样化的多模态推理和文本问答基准上的评估表明，DeAR持续优于最近的基线方法，验证了去中心化和自适应的智能体协作能提升知识密集型推理任务的准确率。

Conclusion: 去中心化的自适应智能体协作可以替代中心化协议，有效提升知识密集型多模态推理任务的性能，为智能体推理系统提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration. DeAR is built on three mechanisms: (1) decentralized capability grounding for query-dependent agent specialization, (2) thought map navigation for targeted peer interactions, and (3) topology update for adaptive error correction. Evaluations across 9 diverse multimodal reasoning and text-based QA benchmarks indicate that DeAR consistently outperforms recent baseline methods, validating that decentralized and adaptive collaboration among agents enhances accuracy in knowledge-intensive reasoning tasks. The source code will be available at https://open_upon_acceptance.

</details>


### [107] [PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17289)
*Dayang Liang,Liyuan He,Xuan Feng,Shuxin Li,Bo An,Yunlong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanPO是一种基于分组规划感知的策略优化方法，通过引入从粗到细的优势信号来区分成功轨迹之间的交互效率差异，从而提升智能体LLM在多轮交互任务中的通用规划能力，相较GRPO平均提升27.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对策略优化（GRPO）变体在训练多轮交互式智能体LLM时，无法区分成功轨迹之间的优势差异——即使这些轨迹在交互效率上差异显著（例如绕远路的成功轨迹与直接高效的成功轨迹被赋予相同的结果奖励），导致优势坍塌和性能瓶颈。

Method: PlanPO提出粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的优势信号：粗粒度层面捕捉同一任务下成功轨迹之间的轨迹级长度差异，细粒度层面捕捉轮次级别响应长度差异。在组相对优化结构内，使智能体从高质量rollout中主动学习具有泛化性和深思熟虑的行为（涵盖交互规划和文本生成），同时避免退化为单纯的长度最小化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld三个具有挑战性的多轮基准上，PlanPO相比GRPO平均提升27.2%，优于近期强基线方法，且额外训练成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: PlanPO通过细粒度的成功轨迹内优势区分，有效解决优势坍塌问题，使智能体学得更高效且可泛化的规划行为，是一种简单有效且成本低廉的RL改进方案。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.

</details>


### [108] [LiveHouse-TS: An Open-world Living Benchmark for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17299)
*Haomin Wen,Ziyu Zhou,Qingxiang Liu,Siru Zhong,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LiveHouse-TS，这是首个面向时间序列基础模型的开放世界实时基准基础设施，通过前推式（prequential）评估方式在真实未来数据上持续评测模型，取代传统静态基准的固定历史窗口快照评估，从而捕捉模型在季节变化、分布漂移和突发事件等真实环境中的长期表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型的评估协议主要依赖固定历史测试窗口的静态基准，只能反映模型在特定历史片段上的平均性能，无法捕捉模型在持续演变的真实世界环境（如季节性变化、分布漂移、突发事件）中的行为变化，缺乏对模型长期时序有效性的评估。

Method: 引入LiveHouse-TS，一个开放世界的实时基准基础设施。其核心方法包括：（1）采用前推式（prequential）评估协议，在真实未来数据上持续评估模型；（2）将时间序列基准测试从快照准确率范式转变为连续时序有效性范式；（3）不局限于一次性排行榜，而是作为持续运行的基础设施，用于探索模型排名长期维持性、分布漂移下的鲁棒性等长期科学问题；（4）在11个领域的17个数据集上进行大规模流式评估。

Result: 在11个领域、17个数据集上的广泛流式评估表明，在实时评估协议下，静态基准中的模型排名发生了剧烈的重新洗牌，说明传统静态评估结果不能可靠地预测模型在动态环境中的长期表现。

Conclusion: LiveHouse-TS证明了从静态快照评估转向连续时序有效性评估的必要性，为时间序列基础模型的长期鲁棒性、分布漂移下的稳定性等关键科学问题提供了新的评估基础设施与研究范式。

Abstract: Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have recently emerged as a highly promising paradigm for cross-domain zero-shot forecasting. However, existing evaluation protocols predominantly rely on static benchmarks with fixed historical test windows. While these benchmarks provide a valuable baseline snapshot, they evaluate an average performance on a fixed history, failing to capture how models behave in continuously evolving real-world environments characterized by seasonal variations, distribution shifts, and unexpected events. To bridge this gap, we introduce LiveHouse-TS, the first open-world living benchmark infrastructure for TSFMs. By evaluating models prequentially on real future data in open-world environments, LiveHouse-TS shifts time series benchmarking from snapshot accuracy to continuous temporal validity. Rather than acting as a one-off leaderboard, our infrastructure serves as a continuous time series infrastructure designed to explore vital, long-term scientific questions: Can model rankings be maintained over the long term? Which models remain genuinely robust under distribution shifts? Extensive streaming evaluations across 11 domains with 17 datasets demonstrate that static rankings undergo a dramatic reshuffling under a live protocol.

</details>


### [109] [SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17301)
*Guozheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了将强化微调策略应用于Qwen2.5-3B-Base模型，以解决研究生级别的无线通信信号数学问题（来自WirelessMATHBench-XL基准）。比较了直接RL与"领域蒸馏CoT SFT + RL"两种训练范式，并评估了GRPO、GSPO和GMPO三种策略，最优模型准确率达39.12%，相较于未训练的Base模型（12.37%）提升了三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然监督式思维链微调和基于可验证奖励的强化学习已显著提升了大语言模型的数学推理能力，但这些方法在信号处理问题上的应用仍然相对未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索将强化微调策略应用于无线通信领域的数学推理问题。

Method: 研究采用两种训练范式：(i)直接在WirelessMATHBench-XL上进行带可验证奖励的强化学习（RL）；(ii)先在蒸馏的无线领域思维链语料上进行监督式微调（SFT），再进行相同的领域特定RL。在两种范式中均对三种RL算法进行基准测试，包括组相对策略优化（GRPO）、组序列策略优化（GSPO）和几何均值策略优化（GMPO）。

Result: 最优模型实现了39.12%的总体准确率，相对于未训练的Base模型（12.37%）获得了超过三倍的提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明领域感知的思维链SFT可以作为后续RL的有效初始化，并评估了GSPO和GMPO相对于GRPO在信号推理任务中是否在稳定性或准确率方面具有优势。

Abstract: Post-training with supervised chain-of-thought fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, their application to signal processing problems remains relatively under-explored. This report investigates reinforcement fine-tuning strategies for adapting Qwen2.5-3B-Base to graduate-level signal mathematical problems from WirelessMATHBench-XL, a comprehensive benchmark for mathematical reasoning in this domain. We examine two training paradigms: (i) direct reinforcement learning (RL) on WirelessMATHBench-XL with verifiable rewards; and (ii) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a distilled wireless-domain chain-of-thought corpus, followed by the same domain-specific RL stage. Across both paradigms, we benchmark Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), and Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO). We aim to assess whether domain-aware CoT SFT serves as an effective initialization for subsequent RL, and whether GSPO or GMPO offer advantages in stability or accuracy over GRPO for signal reasoning tasks. Our best model achieves an overall accuracy of 39.12\%, representing a more than threefold improvement over the untrained Base model (12.37\%).

</details>


### [110] [Wuying-Browser-Agent: Real-World Centric Fundamental Long-Horizon Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17319)
*AIMAE Team,Tianxiang Chen,Yan Cheng,Zhangye Han,Xiaowei Li,Chang Liu,Cheng Liu,Zhongqiang Ma,Long Peng,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Hongliang Wei,Chen Wu,Daiping Xin,Kunyu Zhou,Pengyang Zhou,Peiyuan Chen,Ziyuan Chen,Yutao Deng,Chunyu Dong,Xiangyu Fu,Yicheng Feng,Ruian He,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Zhengqin Liu,Wei Peng,Jinkui Ren,Haoyu Tan,Dong Xiao,Rongkun Xue,Shujian Yang,Xianhang Ye,Ziqi Yuan,Ziyang Yu,Linghan Zhang,Xiantao Zhang,Xuanpu Zhao,Yinan Zhao,Zhenghui Zhao,Bin Zhu,Likai Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wuying-Browser-Agent统一框架，通过执行、监督、优化和评估四个层面的对齐来弥合浏览器智能体在真实部署与演示环境之间的差距，并引入BrowserBench基准（350个双语长任务，平均37.9步），在多个浏览器基准上达到开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体在短而干净的任务上表现良好，但真实部署需要在复杂UI和实时网站上维持数十步决策并从中恢复错误。作者认为仅靠模型规模扩大无法解决这一差距，需要在执行、监督、优化和评估的每个层面进行对齐。

Method: 提出统一框架Wuying-Browser-Agent，包含四个层面：1）结构化浏览器工具（browser harness）提供稳定的执行原语和面向决策的上下文管理；2）RUIC-SFT（反思与UI特化课程SFT）显式训练恢复轨迹和复杂UI交互；3）DAO-GRPO（发散感知在线GRPO）通过基于势函数的奖励塑形和发散感知的步骤加权改进长时程信用分配；4）发布BrowserBench基准（350个双语真实网页任务，平均37.9步）。

Result: Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B在WebVoyager上达到80.6%，Online-Mind2Web上66.7%，BrowserBench上65.1%，刷新浏览器基准开源SOTA。此外，该管线迁移到通用智能体任务，在Tau2-Bench、Claw-Eval和BFCL-v4上平均得分73.8。

Conclusion: 在浏览器智能体管线的每个层面（执行、监督、优化、评估）进行系统化对齐，比单纯扩大模型规模更有效地弥合演示与真实部署的差距；该对齐管线还具备超越浏览器使用场景的通用智能体能力。

Abstract: Browser agents perform well on short, clean demonstrations, but real deployment is fundamentally different: agents must sustain dozens of decisions on live websites while recovering from mistakes and navigating complex UIs. We argue that closing this gap requires alignment at every level of the pipeline, including execution, supervision, optimization, and evaluation, rather than scale alone. We present Wuying-Browser-Agent, a unified framework that addresses each of these levels. A structured browser harness provides stable execution primitives and decision-oriented context management. Reflection and UI-specialized Curriculum SFT (RUIC-SFT) explicitly trains on recovery trajectories and complex-UI interactions. Divergence-Aware Online GRPO (DAO-GRPO) improves long-horizon credit assignment through potential-based reward shaping and divergence-aware step weighting. Finally, we introduce BrowserBench, a bilingual real-web benchmark of 350 tasks averaging 37.9 steps, because most existing benchmarks are too short to expose long-horizon failure modes. Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B achieves 80.6\% on WebVoyager, 66.7\% on Online-Mind2Web, and 65.1\% on BrowserBench, establishing a new open-source state of the art on browser-use benchmarks. The same pipeline also transfers beyond browser use, demonstrating strong general agentic ability and reaching an average score of 73.8 on Tau2-Bench, Claw-Eval, and BFCL-v4.

</details>


### [111] [LLMs for Medical Consultation Are Evaluated Too Late: The Preformulation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17330)
*Yining Hua,Cyrus Ayubcha,Hongbin Na,Levi Lian,Alon Gorenshtein,Yiftach Barash,Eyal Klang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了大型语言模型在医疗咨询中的表现，重点关注"预表述缺口"（preformulation gap）——即模型在患者问题尚未明确时过早给出建议的问题。研究发现，基线条件下9/12的案例模型在患者回答前就给出自我护理或居家管理建议，而指令条件下降为0/12；同时指令条件下结构化的交接总结从0/12提升至10/12。指令虽改善了提问顺序和文档记录，但未能可靠地确保关键事实的获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学咨询LLM评估通常在临床问题已经明确后进行，但真实咨询往往以模糊、淡化或错误框架的主诉开始。作者指出应直接评估模型在首次接触（first-contact）时的可观察行为，而非仅通过诊断准确性或最终答案质量间接推断。

Method: 研究者使用三个API模型、四个医生撰写的多轮对话场景，在基线和"进入照护"（entry-to-care）指令两种条件下进行测试，生成24个固定剧本转录；其中两个案例还使用了自适应标准化病人模拟，额外生成12个转录。通过对比不同条件下模型的提问顺序、建议时机和交接文档生成情况来评估其表现。

Result: 基线条件下9/12的案例-模型组合在患者给出任何回答前就提供了自我护理或居家管理建议，指令条件下降为0/12；而结构化交接总结从基线条件下的0/12提升至指令条件下的10/12。然而指令虽改变了提问顺序和文档记录方式，并未可靠地确保关键决定性事实的获取。

Conclusion: "预表述缺口"应通过可观察的首次接触行为直接评估，而非从诊断准确性或最终答案质量进行推断。当前指令式干预虽能部分改善模型行为，但仍不足以全面解决患者问题表述模糊时的评估缺口。

Abstract: Large language models for medical consultation are often evaluated after a clinical problem has already been made clear, although real consultations may begin with a vague, minimized, or misframed concern. We evaluated three API models across four physician-authored, multi-turn vignettes under baseline and entry-to-care instruction conditions, yielding 24 fixed-script transcripts; two cases also used adaptive standardized-patient simulation, yielding 12 transcripts. Self-care or home-management advice before any patient answer appeared in 9 of 12 baseline case-model cells and 0 of 12 instruction cells, while structured handoff summaries appeared in 0 of 12 and 10 of 12 cells, respectively. The instruction changed sequencing and documentation, although it did not reliably ensure elicitation of decisive facts. The preformulation gap should therefore be evaluated directly through observable first-contact behavior rather than inferred from diagnostic accuracy or final-answer quality.

</details>


### [112] [TileMix: Tile-Centric Mixed-Precision Attention for LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17336)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TileMix是一种面向长上下文预填充的tile级精度路由内核，通过将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的score tiles，并用紧凑bitmask将每个tile组路由至FP16或INT8计算路径，在融合稠密注意力中实现了数值精度作为可执行的空间决策，无需训练即可恢复长上下文质量并提升预填充吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预填充中密集自注意力计算二次方查询-键分数，带来大量计算和内存流量。现有方法要么使用统一的低精度路径，要么选择token交互（稀疏化），但都未能在融合稠密注意力内部对硬件对齐的score tile进行空间精度路由。

Method: TileMix将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的score tiles，将路由决策打包为紧凑bitmask，并将每个tile组通过FP16或INT8分数计算路径进行调度，两条路径共享在线softmax状态。可扩展的精度分组使每个路由bit能管理多个相邻key tile，在长上下文下保持硬件对齐的计算tile和紧凑元数据。通过路由所有合法tile组，TileMix保持稠密token连接性，无需训练，支持GQA、变长批次和INT8 KV缓存。

Result: 在LongEval、LV-Eval和A100预填充基准测试中，针对LLaMA、Qwen和Vicuna模型，TileMix恢复了均匀INT8下丢失的长上下文质量，并相比FP16提升了预填充吞吐量，在不同模型家族上实现了可控的精度-效率平衡前沿。

Conclusion: TileMix通过在融合稠密注意力中对score tiles进行精度路由，提供了一种可控的精度-效率权衡方案，在保持稠密token连接性和无需训练的前提下，兼顾长上下文质量和预填充效率，并在多模型家族上验证了其通用性与有效性。

Abstract: Long-context prefill in large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory traffic because dense self-attention computes quadratic query-key scores. Existing methods either use a uniform low-precision path or select token interactions, leaving spatial precision routing over hardware-aligned score tiles outside fused dense attention. We introduce TileMix, a tile-centric precision-routing kernel that makes numerical precision an executable spatial decision over score-tile groups within fused dense attention.
